Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The segmentation variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 248 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 92 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by segmentation generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 248, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 92. QED.
